You will also receive online lessons, access to Moodle, reimbursement of GPhC, RPS and exam fees, 4 hours of weekly protected study time, and a 2-week paid GP/Hospital placement to enhance clinical knowledge. A 90-hour Designated Prescribing Practitioner setting will be provided to those on the full learning outcomes.
